### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-2 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The card imports the design system through `demo/ui/*` — 19 one-line glass-ui forwarding directories that tranche V's ratified architecture explicitly forbids — while importing the same package directly six lines below in the same file.

**Mechanism.** Aliasing — two names for one concept (edict 2, no aliases; edict 3, no contrivance). It also hides the design-system boundary, which is why L-7's hand-rolled forks survived beside producer atoms.

**Evidence.**

```
PaletteCard.vue:165 `import { Badge } from "../../../../ui/badge"`, :166 `import { Button } from "../../../../ui/button"`, :170 `import { writeClipboard } from "@mkbabb/glass-ui"`, :171 `@mkbabb/glass-ui/motion`; PaletteCardMenu.vue:190 `../../../../ui/dropdown-menu`. `demo/ui/button/index.ts` in full: `export { Button } from "@mkbabb/glass-ui";`. 19 dirs × 1 file each (enumerated). docs/tranches/V/ARCHITECTURE.md §1: "There is no `panes/` dumping ground, `demo/@`, TS/Vite project alias, `@src`, or one-line glass-ui forwarding directory." Debt: 49 demo files import via the barrels vs 14 direct (`grep -rl … | wc -l` = 49; `grep -rn 'from "@mkbabb/glass-ui"' demo --include=*.vue | wc -l` = 14). `git log -- demo/ui/button/index.ts` → f2c8f565 (glass-ui 7 adoption), a61094e3 (demo/@ dies).
```

**Reproduction.** `for d in demo/ui/*/; do echo "$d $(ls $d)"; done` → 19 directories, each containing only index.ts, each a single re-export line from @mkbabb/glass-ui. Compare PaletteCard.vue:165-171 for both paths in one file.

**Proposed cure.** Delete `demo/ui/` entirely; rewrite the 49 importers to the bare `@mkbabb/glass-ui` specifier. Zero runtime delta (the barrels are pure re-exports), −19 directories, and every producer-atom consumption becomes visible in one grep.

---

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-3 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** PaletteCard is consumed across feature boundaries (workbenches/mix, workbenches/extract) in violation of the ratified import lattice, and its 16 optional emits are wired 0/16, 4/16, 6/16, 15/16 by its four hosts — so it renders Delete/Export/Publish controls that provably do nothing. In Mix the card is additionally nested inside a native <button>, whose content model forbids interactive descendants.

**Mechanism.** Wrong direction of dependency. A palettes-feature component that three features must render was made optional-everything and pushed outward as 16 emits; the component therefore cannot know whether an action is serviceable and renders controls it cannot honour.

**Evidence.**

```
ARCHITECTURE.md §1: "Cross-feature internal imports are forbidden by construction" + the lattice `feature → color-session / own descendants / platform / shared / published packages`. Violating edges: MixSourceSelector.vue:8 `import { PaletteCard, PaletteColorStrip } from "../../palettes/browser/card"`, ExtractWorkbench.vue:197-200. Measured binding table (emits from PaletteCard.vue:200-218 tested against each `<PaletteCard …>` tag): BrowsePane.vue 15/16, PalettesPane.vue 6/16, ExtractWorkbench.vue 4/16, MixSourceSelector.vue 0/16. PaletteCardMenu gates items only on paletteKind/isOwned/isAdmin (PaletteCardMenu.vue:16,28,49,74,134), never on host capability. Nesting: MixSourceSelector.vue:246-267 `<button …><PaletteCard …/></button>` wrapping PaletteCard.vue:96-104 `<Button icon-only aria-label="Palette menu">`.
```

**Reproduction.** Static and deterministic: `MixSourceSelector.vue:264-267` passes only `:palette` and `:css-color`; PaletteCard's menu still renders Save/Publish/Rename/Export×5/Delete for a `saved` palette; an emit with no listener is a no-op. (Live confirmation of the nested-button click bubbling was blocked by L-14's route drift — that half is a hypothesis; the unlistened-emit half is not.)

**Proposed cure.** Split presentation from capability: a `shared/ui/SwatchCard.vue` (colors + slots, zero Palette knowledge, zero actions) consumed directly by mix/extract — the cross-feature edge disappears — and a feature-owned `PaletteCard` that binds the Palette DTO plus the existing injected ports (usePalettePorts.ts:138-192) to it, building the menu from a capability list the port supplies so unserviceable actions are absent, not dead. Emits collapse 16 → 1.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-4 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The menu↔card action channel is an untyped `string` bus with a silent-drop default, so the two halves cannot be related by the compiler; it already carries a dead handler.

**Mechanism.** God-module symptom relocated, not cured: an 18-entry dispatch table in the root file, coupled to a sibling by string literals that no tool checks.

**Evidence.**

```
PaletteCardMenu.vue:225 `action: [action: string]`; PaletteCard.vue:293-319 `const actions: Record<string, () => void> = {…}` then `const fn = actions[action]; if (!fn) return;`. Measured set diff: menu emits 17 strings, card handles 18; `handled but NEVER emitted: ['copyAll']`. `grep -rn "copyAll" demo/` returns exactly one line — PaletteCard.vue:294.
```

**Reproduction.** node script extracting `$emit('action','X')` literals from PaletteCardMenu.vue and the `X: () =>` keys from PaletteCard.vue's handler record: emitted-but-unhandled = [], handled-but-never-emitted = ['copyAll'].

**Proposed cure.** Delete the bus. The menu receives `actions: readonly CardAction[]` (`{id,label,icon,disabled?,run()}`) built once in a `useCardActions.ts` from the port, and renders `v-for`. No strings, no orphan possible, no silent drop, and PaletteCardMenu's 228 lines of v-if policy collapse into a list renderer.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-5 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** Feedback display state lives in the card but is driven by an imperative `defineExpose` handle harvested through `(el: any)` ref maps in two panes; the handle has leaked into a composable's dependency contract.

**Mechanism.** Inverted ownership: the pane knows the outcome of every mutation, the card owns the state that displays it. Bridging that gap costs an imperative handle, a per-row instance map (never pruned — the `el && …` guard skips the null unmount callback) and two `any` casts that erase the instance type.

**Evidence.**

```
PaletteCard.vue:233-244 (`feedbackMessage/Variant/Visible` refs + `defineExpose({ showFeedback })`); BrowsePane.vue:94 `:ref="(el: any) => el && (cardRefs[palette.slug] = el)"`, :209 `reactive<Record<string, InstanceType<typeof PaletteCard>>>({})`, :230/:239/:249/:264 `showFeedback(...)`; PalettesPane.vue:84/:177/:207; useDialogBrowseActions.ts:36-44 documents `onForkError?` as routing onto "the palette card's showFeedback".
```

**Reproduction.** Static: 7 call sites listed above; `cardRefs` entries for removed palettes are retained because the unmount invocation passes `el === null` and the guard short-circuits before deletion.

**Proposed cure.** Make it data: hold the per-palette operation result in the port (`feedback: Record<slug, {message,variant}|null>`) and pass `:feedback` down as a prop. `defineExpose`, both `any` casts, both `cardRefs` maps and the `onForkError?` indirection all die.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-6 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The six-file folder is a god module distributed across files, not a decomposition along seams: the root keeps every piece of state and PaletteCardSwatches is a markup shard taking 8 props / re-emitting 8 events with zero state of its own.

**Mechanism.** The seam was cut through the state rather than around it. The one real force (useHeightTransition's onBeforeCollapse closes the popover — PaletteCard.vue:276-278) argues for moving BOTH into the expandable child, not for hoisting one into the parent.

**Evidence.**

```
Per-file measure — PaletteCard.vue: 364 L, 10 props, 16 emits, 4 refs + 3 composables + an 18-entry dispatch table; PaletteCardSwatches.vue: 8 props (:75-84 `colors,isLocal,displaySlug,safeFirstColor,openPopoverIndex,canHover,floatingStyle,swatchClass`), 8 emits (:86-95), 0 state; PaletteCardMeta 1/1/0; PaletteRenameInput 1/2/2; ActionFeedback 4/1/1. Four of the swatch props and four of its emits exist only because the root calls `useHoverPopover()` on its behalf (PaletteCard.vue:246-255 ↔ PaletteCard.vue:139-157). The sibling composable useSwatchActions.ts:30-40 calls `useHoverPopover()` itself, proving the child could.
```

**Reproduction.** Static: prop/emit counts above; `wc -l` shows the root is still the largest file by 1.6× after five extractions.

**Proposed cure.** Re-cut on the three seams the DOM already has: PaletteCardHeader (handle/title/rename/badges/meta/menu trigger), PaletteCardBody (the expandable region, owning useHoverPopover AND the collapse motion, emitting only addColor/editColor/copyColor), and a root that owns only press + expanded + the DTO. Body props 8→3, emits 8→3, root loses 4 refs and a composable.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-7 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** A hand-rolled icon-button recipe is duplicated 16× across the demo (4× inside this component folder) while the same component uses the glass-ui Button atom for its menu trigger six lines away — a dual path for one concept, and the fork's ~26px target is below the tap-target floor.

**Mechanism.** Dual path / per-instance styling: the producer atom and a 10-utility local fork coexist inside one folder, breaking edict 4 (variants belong in glass-ui) and edict 5 (root-level styling, never per-instance overrides).

**Evidence.**

```
PaletteCard.vue:93-104 carries the ratified cure in comment form ("S.W5-4: 3rd copy of the hand-rolled icon-trigger recipe dies onto the glass-ui atom; the sm square also cures the ~24px touch target") and uses `<Button icon-only variant="ghost" size="sm">`. The fork survives at PaletteCardSwatches.vue:14,41,49,56; PaletteCardMeta.vue:45; PaletteRenameInput.vue:20,26 — `class="p-1.5 rounded-sm hover:bg-accent active:scale-95 active:bg-accent/70 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/40"`. Measured: `grep -ro "active:bg-accent/70 transition-colors" demo --include=*.vue | wc -l` = 16, across 6 files.
```

**Reproduction.** `grep -rl "active:bg-accent/70" demo --include="*.vue"` → PaletteSlugBar.vue, PaletteCardMeta.vue, CurrentPaletteEditor.vue, PaletteRenameInput.vue, PaletteCardSwatches.vue, AdminTagsPanel.vue.

**Proposed cure.** Substitute all 16 sites with `<Button icon-only variant="ghost" size="sm" :aria-label="…">`. If the swatch popover row genuinely needs a smaller square, add a `size="xs"` variant in glass-ui — producer-side, one place — never a local class list.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure (module boundaries, ownershi` · L-8 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** Two `slugify` implementations own one concept inside the same feature, both reachable from the card's Export action, producing measurably different output for the same palette name.

**Mechanism.** Duplicate semantic ownership: one concept, two homes, divergent behaviour — so a palette stored as `cafe-noir` downloads as `caf-noir.json` with `--palette-caf-noir-*` custom properties.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/palettes/export.ts:9-11 (lowercase + `[^a-z0-9]+` → '-') vs demo/palettes/utils.ts:3-12 (NFKD normalize, strip combining marks, then slugify) — the latter is what `createSlug` mints store ids with (utils.ts:14-16, usePaletteStore.ts:4). Measured: "Café Noir!" → export.ts `caf-noir` vs utils.ts `cafe-noir`; "Ω palette" → `palette` vs `-palette`. Path from the card: PaletteCard.vue:308-312 (`export` emit) → BrowsePane.vue:324 / PalettesPane.vue:211 `usePaletteExport()` → usePaletteExport.ts:15-19 → export.ts.
```

**Reproduction.** node with both function bodies verbatim: `"Café Noir!" export.ts=> "caf-noir"  utils.ts=> "cafe-noir"`. (Note: the brief's named suspect `src/export/serializers` does not exist — `ls src/` → color css easing.ts foundation quantize.ts subpaths transform value.ts.)

**Proposed cure.** One `palettes/slug.ts` exporting `slugify` (the NFKD form) + `createSlug`; export.ts imports it. Fix the leading-hyphen bug for non-Latin names once, in the single home. Also surface `usePaletteExport.ts:21-23`'s swallowed failure through the ActionFeedback chip the card already has.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/picker/ColorPic` · C-5 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** The Cmd+K handler tests global modifier STATE (`keys.cmd?.value && keys.k?.value`) rather than the event, so any key pressed while Cmd and K are physically held is preventDefault'd and re-toggles the dropdown. There is also no `e.repeat` guard.

**Mechanism.** Chord detection via sampled global key state instead of event inspection — the predicate is true for the whole duration the keys are held, not for the one event that is the chord.

**Evidence.**

```
ColorPicker.vue:263-266. Live probe: hold Meta, hold k (dropdown opens, aria-expanded="true"), then press `a` -> {"A_afterKdown":"true","A_afterOtherKey":"false","A_events":[{"k":"a","meta":true,"rep":false,"dp":true}]}. Cmd+A (Select All) is swallowed AND the color-space dropdown toggles shut on an unrelated keystroke.
```

**Reproduction.** On http://localhost:9000/#/ : page.keyboard.down('Meta'); page.keyboard.down('k'); (dropdown opens) ; page.keyboard.down('a'); page.keyboard.up('a'). The keydown for `a` reports defaultPrevented=true and the trigger's aria-expanded flips true->false.

**Proposed cure.** Test the event: `if (e.key === "k" && (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && !e.repeat)`. The `useMagicKeys()` import (ColorPicker.vue:123,247) then has no remaining consumer and is deleted with it.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/picker/ColorPic` · C-6 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** The four channel slider thumbs are 12 CSS px wide at every viewport — WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8 (Target Size Minimum, 24x24) fails four times. The picker contributes half of the entire application's small-tap-target count on all 60 audit captures.

**Mechanism.** Hit-area extension applied to the wrong box: the pseudo-element grows the track root on one axis only, while the element carrying role="slider" and the actual grab affordance is the 12px thumb.

**Evidence.**

```
Measured live over the `.pane-shell` subtree at 390x600: [{"tag":"span","role":"slider","w":12,"h":24,"name":"L channel"}, ...A, B, ALPHA identical]. These are exactly the rows in docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/visual/REPORT.json for `/` desktop ({"w":12,"h":24,"tag":"span","label":"L channel"} x4 of 8 smallTapTargets) and mobile ({"w":12,"h":44} x4) — REPORT.md:90-151 shows 8 on every one of the 60 captures. The touch rung at demo/picker/controls/ComponentSliders/ComponentSliders.vue:345-357 grows the SliderRoot's vertical hit box (`block-size: max(100%, 2.75rem)`, `inset-inline: 0`) and never touches the thumb, which is why h goes 24->44 on coarse pointers while w stays 12.
```

**Reproduction.** Open http://localhost:9000/#/ and evaluate `[...document.querySelectorAll('.channel-slider [role="slider"]')].map(e=>{const r=e.getBoundingClientRect();return {w:r.width,h:r.height,name:e.getAttribute('aria-label')}})` — four entries, all w=12.

**Proposed cure.** Add an inline hit extension on the thumb (`::before` with `inline-size: max(100%, 24px)`), and put it in glass-ui's spectrum-slider primitive rather than as a fifth demo override in ComponentSliders.vue's already-unscoped style block (owner edicts 4 and 5). No wave currently owns this: W48 §Work-4 covers aria-valuetext and meter live-ness but not target size, and W46's PR-12 names Dock/tab/swatch glyphs, not sliders.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/picker/ColorPic` · C-7 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** `useHeaderCondense` is structurally unreachable: its sufficiency gate demands more scroll overflow than the picker card can ever produce, because the header is itself the dominant term in the card's height. 127 LoC plus six CSS rule blocks are dead at every witnessed viewport.

**Mechanism.** A self-defeating predicate: the gate refuses to condense unless overflow exceeds roughly half the header, but the header is the largest single block in the scroll content, so that threshold outruns the residual overflow at every band where the picker is usable.

**Evidence.**

```
ColorPicker.vue:186-195 wires it; the gate is demo/picker/composables/useHeaderCondense.ts:93-99 (`savings = expandedH - (condensedH || expandedH * 0.5)`; `if (overflow <= savings + threshold) return`). Measured live at 390x600, the tightest band that produces overflow at all: {"viewport":[390,600],"expandedHeaderH":184,"overflow":64,"threshold":16,"gateRequires_overflow_gt":108,"gateOpens":false}. At the audit's own reference sizes the overflow is ZERO: {"A_freshMobile_before":{"cardOverflowY":"auto","scrollHeight":586,"clientHeight":586},"B_desktop":{"cardOverflowY":"visible","scrollHeight":683,"clientHeight":683}}. Scrolling the card its full 64px (scrollTop=64) leaves condensedClass=false. Dead surface: useHeaderCondense.ts (127 lines), demo/picker/header.css lines 75/82/100/116/128, the sentinel div at ColorPicker.vue:12, the $el-unwrapping computed at ColorPicker.vue:189-192, the class binding at ColorPicker.vue:25.
```

**Reproduction.** Open http://localhost:9000/#/ at 390x600, then evaluate: `const c=document.querySelector('.pane-shell .card'), h=document.querySelector('.picker-header'); ({overflow:c.scrollHeight-c.clientHeight, H:h.getBoundingClientRect().height, required:h.getBoundingClientRect().height*0.5+16})` -> overflow 64, H 184, required 108. Set c.scrollTop=300, wait 900ms: `.picker-header` still has no `is-condensed` class.

**Proposed cure.** Delete it (owner edicts 2 and 3). If the whole-header contraction is still wanted, it needs a mechanism that is not 'condense only when there is enough overflow to sustain condensing'. W48 orders deleting the seat.css:88 title-row reservation and the readoutReservation two-line lock but leaves this apparatus standing.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/picker/ColorPic` · C-8 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** The picker cancels its parent's shared, app-wide debounced writes on its own unmount — an ownership inversion that silently drops in-flight color changes belonging to sibling components.

**Mechanism.** A child disposing state it did not create: cleanup responsibility placed on the consumer instead of the owning composable's own scope.

**Evidence.**

```
ColorPicker.vue:381-385 calls `parseAndSetColorDebounced.cancel()` and `updateColorComponentDebounced.cancel()`. Both are created by useColorPipeline in App.vue (useColorPipeline.ts:92 and :209) and provided app-wide via COLOR_MODEL_KEY; other consumers are ComponentSliders.vue:115, ConsoleRail.vue:115, HeroBlob.vue:56 and demo/shell/dock/ColorInput.vue. The pane KeepAlive is `:max="6"` (App.vue:104) over 7 distinct left panes (color-picker, browse, extract, generate, gradient, atmosphere, admin-*), so the picker IS LRU-evictable. The parse debounce window is 2000ms (useColorParsing.ts:92).
```

**Reproduction.** NONE — hypothesis on the observable drop. The ownership violation itself is direct from the cited lines; the concrete drop requires evicting the picker from the 6-slot LRU while a 2000ms parse debounce is pending, which I did not drive.

**Proposed cure.** The pipeline owns its own lifetime: move both cancels into an `onScopeDispose` inside useColorPipeline. The picker should cancel nothing it did not create — and should instead be clearing the two timers it DOES own (see C-14), which it does not.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defects in demo/picker/ColorPic` · C-9 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** Vacuous test gate: the only test in the repository that references ColorPicker.vue does not mount it — it readFileSyncs the file and asserts two regexes over its text. No test anywhere drives the readout edit path, the keyboard shortcut, the edit state machine or the numeric commit path.

**Mechanism.** Grep-over-source-text standing in for behavioural coverage — the retired `proof:*` idiom the owner deleted as 'overfit junk' (memory: feedback-proof-idiom-retired.md), reappearing under a different filename.

**Evidence.**

```
test/picker-blob-config.test.ts:12 reads the file; its only two assertions about it are line 44 `expect(picker).not.toMatch(/<HeroBlob[^>]*@click=/)` and line 49 `expect(picker.match(/writeClipboard\(/g)).toHaveLength(1)`. Run: `npx vitest run test/picker-blob-config.test.ts --reporter=basic` -> ` ✓ test/picker-blob-config.test.ts (3 tests) 2ms / Test Files 1 passed (1) / Tests 3 passed (3)`. Grepping test/ and e2e/ for `readout-fig`, `contenteditable` or a Cmd+K chord returns only e2e/smoke/flows/color-propose.spec.ts, whose own comments (lines 17-22) scope it to the dock's propose-mode span. e2e/smoke/oracles/readout-seam.spec.ts measures box geometry only (boxTop/boxBottom/minHeightPx) and would pass unchanged with C-1 present.
```

**Reproduction.** Exact mutations that keep the suite green: (a) delete `handleKeydown` and both window.addEventListener/removeEventListener lines; (b) delete the entire `onComponentInput` hex branch (ColorPicker.vue:225-229); (c) replace `parseAndSetColor(newVal)` in the inputColor watcher (ColorPicker.vue:360) with a no-op; (d) delete both onUnmounted debounce cancels; (e) invert `isEditing` (ColorPicker.vue:273). Each passes, because the two assertions look only for `<HeroBlob @click=` and count `writeClipboard(`.

**Proposed cure.** Mount the component (@vue/test-utils with a provided COLOR_MODEL_KEY stub) and assert behaviour: readout-model agreement after a manual edit followed by an independent color change; cross-channel commit independence; listener removal on deactivate; a throwing `originalCss` leaving the edit machine consistent. Delete the two regex assertions.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-05 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** The P019 identity/specimen type pair is at the required 1/√φ only at the phone floor; it drifts to near-parity at 1440, so at desktop the label and the numeric specimen are optically the same size.

**Mechanism.** Two independently clamped sources with different binding arms cannot hold a constant ratio. The register (V-A140) describes this as a fixed two-rung skip; the rendered truth is a ratio that is exactly right at one end of the band and collapses at the other — worse than documented.

**Evidence.**

```
Measured label font-size / headline font-size: 390x844 → 32.928 / 41.888 = 0.7861 (required 1/√φ = 0.78615, correct); 1440x900 → 53.280 / 54.644 = 0.9750. Drift +24.0% across the band. Sources: label = --type-display-2 = clamp(2.058rem, 1.5rem + 2.2vw, 3.33rem) at ColorSpaceSelector.vue:209 (ceiling-bound at 1440); headline = calc(min(var(--type-display-4), max(11.65cqi, 2.618rem)) * var(--readout-fit,1)) at ColorComponentDisplay.vue:137-140 (floor-bound at 390, 11.65cqi-bound at 1440 — --type-display-4 resolves to 86.11px and never binds). VISUAL-CONSTITUTION §3.2: "BI P019 supplies one paired proportional clamp; both sizes resolve from that one clamp … rather than only at endpoints" and "independently clamped display-2/display-3 tokens and a local approximation are forbidden."
```

**Reproduction.** On the settled / route read parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.space-trigger')).fontSize) / parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.readout')).fontSize) at 390x844 (0.7861) and at 1440x900 (0.9750).

**Proposed cure.** One shared P019 paired clamp: derive both arms from a single fluid quantity so label = headline / √φ identically through floor, fluid and ceiling. Delete the local 11.65cqi mint and the independent --type-display-2 claim; the pair becomes one token consumption, not two.

---

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-06 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** The numeric headline renders in Fraunces, not Fira Code, so the identity arm and the specimen arm share a family — which is why D-05's near-parity reads as a collision rather than a contrast.

**Mechanism.** The family split that carries the identity/value distinction was never applied to the headline; the whole readout inherits font-display (Fraunces) from ColorComponentDisplay.vue:14.

**Evidence.**

```
getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.readout')).fontFamily = 'Fraunces, "Fraunces Fallback", serif' at both 1440 and 390. The .fira-code class is applied only when formatted[component]?.monospace (ColorComponentDisplay.vue:28), which is false for every numeric channel. VISUAL-CONSTITUTION §3.2: "the read-only Fira Code numeric readout is the headline and the Fraunces selected-space identity is exactly one adjacent golden rung smaller"; §4: "value applies Fraunces to its identity arm and Fira Code to its headline arm at exact 1/√φ"; §7 Picker repeats it.
```

**Reproduction.** On the settled / route, read getComputedStyle('.readout').fontFamily; confirm no .fira-code class on any .readout-fig by inspecting the rendered innerHTML of a .readout-cell.

**Proposed cure.** Apply Fira Code to the headline arm unconditionally as §4's sole paired-scale exception specifies, landing atomically with the D-05 paired clamp — family and ratio are one design decision, not two.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-07 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** The flagship route has zero <h1>, and the first heading in the document is an <h3> whose text is a number tuple and whose children are three interactive textboxes.

**Mechanism.** A display-sized readout was expressed with a heading primitive because CardTitle was the nearest available typographic slot; the route's actual identity heading was never authored.

**Evidence.**

```
Live at 1440x900: h1count = 0; headings[0] = { tag: "H3", data-slot: "card-title", text: "64.8%,18.0,42.0" }; contenteditable count = 3, all role="textbox", all inside that H3; main count = 1 (this half is correct). ColorComponentDisplay.vue:13 renders the readout as glass-ui <CardTitle>, which emits <h3>. VISUAL-CONSTITUTION §4.1: "Each route has one H1 and exactly one stable main landmark"; §5.1: "the single visible H1 exist[s] before app-ready". PROPORTION-AUDIT §5.11: "A display-sized readout is not therefore a document heading or live status … route H1 owns heading hierarchy."
```

**Reproduction.** On the settled / route: document.querySelectorAll('h1').length → 0; [...document.querySelectorAll('h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6')][0] → H3[data-slot=card-title] with numeric text; document.querySelectorAll('[contenteditable="true"]').length → 3, all descendants of that H3.

**Proposed cure.** Shell owns one route <h1> per §5.1's focus/announcement table; the readout becomes <output> (non-heading, non-live, labelled) per §5.11's "one labeled non-live domain output". Lands with D-01 as one atomic change — the heading role and the editor role are the same fusion.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-08 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** Card is used as the route chassis where the canon binds InstrumentChassis (which glass-ui 7.0.0 ships), and a second Card is nested inside it — falsifying the premise PROPORTION-AUDIT §2 ruling 2 is conditioned on.

**Mechanism.** The producer chassis was never consumed, so every quantity it would supply (title gap, stage/inspector ratio, boundary set, reserve) is re-minted locally — which is the upstream cause of D-02's undefined token, D-04's equal grid and D-05's independent clamps. Owner edict 4: glass-ui is the design system; do not hand-roll what it provides.

**Evidence.**

```
Live: the picker plate is <div data-slot="card" class="glass-resting card rounded-card …">, composed at ColorPicker.vue:6,21,64 from Card + CardHeader + CardContent. `node -p "Object.keys(require('@mkbabb/glass-ui/package.json').exports)" | grep chassis` → ./instrument-chassis; `grep -rl InstrumentChassis node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui/dist/` → dist/instrument-chassis.js, dist/components/instrument-chassis/InstrumentChassis.vue.d.ts. Nested Card: ComponentSliders.vue:25 renders <Card surface="veil">, measured live as a glass-quiet card at 469 x 190.4 inside the glass-resting card. `--instrument-title-gap` computes to "" on the live page. VISUAL-CONSTITUTION §3.1 binds Picker's outer housing to InstrumentChassis and states Card "is never the default page primitive"; §2 "One surface has one tier. An inner card is not automatically another pane of glass." PROPORTION-AUDIT §2 ruling 2 opens: "Because Picker no longer nests a Card, BI P122 exposes --instrument-title-gap …" — false at HEAD.
```

**Reproduction.** On the settled / route: getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--instrument-title-gap') → ""; document.querySelectorAll('.pane-shell [data-slot=card]').length → 2 (glass-resting outer, glass-quiet inner at 469x190.4). In the tree: grep -n 'Card' demo/picker/controls/ComponentSliders/ComponentSliders.vue → line 25 <Card surface="veil">.

**Proposed cure.** Transpose the picker to the producer InstrumentChassis (stage / inspector / action regions, P122 boundary set [] and reserve none per OPTICAL-BENCH-COMPOSITIONS §5) and collapse the inner veil Card to a plain material region — §2's "one surface has one tier". The proportion, title-gap and pair rows then close by token consumption instead of five separate local cures. PROPORTION-AUDIT §2 ruling 2 must be re-premised before it is executed.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-09 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** The "ONE rhythm law" token introduced to cure a 6× title→readout gap divergence did not cure it — it inverted the sign and left a 2.75× divergence, now worst at desktop.

**Mechanism.** A token was introduced against a symptom whose cause lives in a different file: the seam is governed by the readout's two-line reservation (D-02), not by the header row-gap. Curing the wrong quantity leaves the divergence intact and changes only which arm is worse.

**Evidence.**

```
header.css:18-23 states the token replaces "the fixed gap-y-1 (4px) that … read as a 6× divergent gap (4.0px@1440 vs 24.6px@390)" so that "1440 and 390 share ONE rhythm SOURCE". Measured label→headline separation after the cure: 1440 → 69.93px, 390 → 25.40px, ratio 2.75× with the loose arm now at desktop (previously 6.15× with the loose arm at mobile). The row-gap token itself is well-behaved: getComputedStyle('.picker-header').rowGap = 7.168px at 1440.
```

**Reproduction.** Measure the label→headline ink separation (Range.getClientRects over .space-trigger and .readout) at 1440x900 (69.93px) and 390x844 (25.40px); separately read getComputedStyle('.picker-header').rowGap (7.168px at 1440) to show the token is not the governing quantity.

**Proposed cure.** Retire --picker-header-rhythm in favour of the producer P122 --instrument-title-gap once the chassis lands (D-08), and delete the readout reservation (D-02). The seam then has exactly one owner and the two arms converge by construction.

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-10 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** The only keyboard-operable channel control is a 12x24 CSS px target at every viewport — half the WCAG 2.2 minimum on the inline axis, with no touch accommodation.

**Mechanism.** The visible thumb glyph IS the hit target — the three quantities §5.7 separates were collapsed into one, so the only way to meet the floor would be to fatten the optic, and neither was done.

Live measurement of focusables inside the picker Card: 1440x900 → L/A/B/ALPHA channel sliders 12.0 x 24 CSS px; 390x844 → 12.1 x 24 CSS px. WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8 (Target Size, Minimum, AA) floors at 24 x 24 CSS px. PROPORTION-AUDIT §5.7: "Visual glyph size, operable target size and layout reservation are separate quantities. Accessibility floors do not require bloated visible chrome"; PR-12 disposes this family as TIGHTEN with "invisible/seat geometry preserves target floor while optics follow rung".
```

**Reproduction.** At 390x844 and 1440x900 on the settled / route, map every focusable inside document.querySelector('.pane-shell [data-slot=card]') to its getBoundingClientRect() and filter width < 24 || height < 24 → four [role=slider] thumbs at 12.0–12.1 x 24 in both matrices.

**Proposed cure.** Give the producer Slider thumb an invisible seat (pseudo-element or padded hit box) at ≥24x24 while the painted glyph keeps its 12px optical rung — exactly PR-12's disposition. Root-level, in glass-ui's Slider, not per-instance in demo/picker (owner edict 5).

### `CHALLENGE-D — design (visual truth, state coverage, motion, ` · D-12 · CHALLENGE-D

**Defect.** An undiscoverable global Cmd+K shortcut is bound on `window` with no target guard and no affordance anywhere in the UI.

**Mechanism.** Three defects in one binding: undiscoverable (no affordance), unscoped (a bare window listener fires while focus is in any editable surface, including this component's own contenteditable cells and the dock colour input), and a toggle on a held-key predicate (useMagicKeys reports cmd and k as held, so any subsequent keydown while both remain down re-toggles the popover).

**Evidence.**

```
demo/picker/ColorPicker.vue:247 (const keys = useMagicKeys()), :263-266 (if (keys.cmd?.value && keys.k?.value) { e.preventDefault(); selectedColorSpaceOpen.value = !selectedColorSpaceOpen.value; }), registered at :377 (window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeydown)). No tooltip, menu item, hint or label names it anywhere in demo/picker/. PROPORTION-AUDIT PR-07 ("Hover-only/unlabeled controls") and §5.6 ("Subtraction precedes explanation"); VISUAL-CONSTITUTION §5 interaction grammar select → tune → commit.
```

**Reproduction.** Read demo/picker/ColorPicker.vue:247,263-266,377 — the handler has no event.target / activeElement check and no document-scope guard. Reproduction of the re-toggle: hold Cmd+K and press any third key; the predicate is still true and the popover toggles again.

**Proposed cure.** Either give the shortcut a named, visible home (a labelled affordance on the selector trigger, or a command palette that owns the binding) and scope the listener to the instrument root with an editable-target guard, or delete it. Subtraction precedes explanation: an undiscoverable command is not part of the grammar.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-3 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** Four of ColorPicker.vue's import edges violate the repository's own declared import-direction law in docs/tranches/V/ARCHITECTURE.md §1 (lines 47-58) — feature->app, feature->shell, feature->another feature's internal, plus a forwarding directory forbidden by name.

**Mechanism.** Layer inversion: a route leaf reaches upward into boot, shell and a sibling feature for state that belongs in color-session or the shell.

**Evidence.**

```
Law: ARCHITECTURE.md:47-58 `feature -> color-session / own descendants / platform / shared / published packages` and line 58 'Cross-feature internal imports are forbidden by construction'; line 39 'There is no ... one-line glass-ui forwarding directory'. Violations: ColorPicker.vue:108 `../ui/card` (forwarding dir); :129 `../color-picker/composables/boot/useOverture` (feature->app composition root; demo/color-picker/ holds App.vue, router/, index.html, boot/ and is not even the declared name — the tree says demo/app/); :130 `../shell/useViewManager` (feature->shell; used only for paletteActive at :313); :131 `../palettes/usePalettePorts` (cross-feature internal; used only for paletteManager.commitColorEdit at :294 — a palette-domain write executed from inside the picker). Directory-level cycle: App.vue:164 imports ../picker, usePaletteWiring.ts:21 imports its type, while ColorPicker.vue:129 imports back into color-picker/.
```

**Reproduction.** cd /Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js && sed -n '108,141p' demo/picker/ColorPicker.vue && sed -n '47,58p' docs/tranches/V/ARCHITECTURE.md

**Proposed cure.** Overture beat B4 arrives as a shell-owned provide (or the shell owns the reveal class outright). paletteActive is computed in the Dock. The edit-commit inverts: the picker emits an intent, color-session's edit session owns the commit, palettes/ subscribes — the picker never names palettes/ again.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-4 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** usePaletteWiring routes four cross-cutting colour operations through the picker COMPONENT INSTANCE and silently swaps to different semantics when it is absent — three masking fallbacks plus a 2-second polling retry with a give-up console.warn.

**Mechanism.** Dual path with masking fallback — the same public operation resolves to two different implementations depending on whether an unrelated component happens to be mounted.

**Evidence.**

```
demo/color-picker/composables/usePaletteWiring.ts:65-73 emitApply — picker present -> onPaletteApply(colors) (replaces savedColors with ALL parsed colours); absent -> applyColorString(colors[0]) (sets the CURRENT colour to the first entry, touches no palette). :121-127 emitSetCurrentColor — applyExternalColor vs applyColorString; these are two near-identical implementations in one file (useColorPipeline.ts:182-188 and :247-257): one calls setCurrentColor (refreshing stableHue) and throws on parse failure, the other calls updateModel and swallows the error. :33-58 whenColorPickerReady polls 40 x 50ms then console.warn('gave up waiting for the color picker to mount'), which on mobile ALWAYS exhausts, so emitStartEdit (:106-119) is dead. All of these are pure pipeline calls: App.vue:245 already holds `pipeline` directly, and 5 of ColorPicker's 12 defineExpose members are straight re-exports of inject(COLOR_MODEL_KEY).
```

**Reproduction.** Read demo/color-picker/composables/usePaletteWiring.ts:33-127 alongside demo/color-session/useColorPipeline.ts:182-188 and :247-257. Behavioural repro: at 390x844 (where colorPickerRef is permanently null per L-2) applying a saved palette loads only its first colour into the current swatch instead of replacing savedColors, and starting a palette colour edit logs the give-up warning after ~2s.

**Proposed cure.** Delete the picker parameter, the retry loop and both fallback arms; call the pipeline directly. Then reconcile applyExternalColor and applyColorString into one named operation in color-session.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-5 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The picker registers a window-level keydown listener in onMounted and releases it only in onUnmounted, but is rendered inside <KeepAlive>, so leaving the picker view deactivates rather than unmounts it and the global shortcut stays live on every other route.

**Mechanism.** Wrong lifecycle pair under KeepAlive: onMounted/onUnmounted instead of onActivated/onDeactivated, in a component whose owner caches it.

**Evidence.**

```
ColorPicker.vue:376-379 onMounted -> window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown); :381-385 onUnmounted -> removeEventListener + two debounce cancels. PaneSlot.vue:120 wraps the pane in <KeepAlive :max="max">. MEASURED live at 1440x900: (1) on #/ fire Cmd+K -> 'Select color space' aria-expanded='true' and [role=listbox] present; (2) Cmd+K again -> closed; (3) navigate to #/browse -> the picker Select is absent from the DOM (pane deactivated); (4) fire Cmd+K on #/browse; (5) navigate back to #/ -> aria-expanded='true', [role=listbox] present. The deactivated picker consumed a global shortcut from a route it does not own. The same hole leaves parseAndSetColorDebounced (2000ms) and updateColorComponentDebounced (500ms) uncancelled on every view switch. Compounding: two keyboard mechanisms for one shortcut set — useMagicKeys() at :247 plus the raw window handler that reads magic-keys state at :263.
```

**Reproduction.** With the dev server on :9000 at 1440x900, in the page context: navigate to '#/', dispatch KeyboardEvent keydown {key:'Meta'} then {key:'k',metaKey:true} on window -> the colour-space Select opens; dispatch again to close; set location.hash='#/browse', wait for the swap, dispatch the same pair; set location.hash='#/' -> the Select is open again (aria-expanded='true').

**Proposed cure.** The shortcut is app-level: move it to a shell-owned keyboard-map composable registered once at the composition root that dispatches to the active instrument. The picker keeps no window listener. Any listener that must live in a KeepAlive'd leaf uses onActivated/onDeactivated.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-6 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** demo/ui/ is 19 one-line glass-ui forwarding directories with 90 import sites — a directory level the repository's own architecture forbids by name — and it creates a de-facto dual spelling of the same package.

**Mechanism.** A pure indirection layer surviving from a retired shadcn-local-copy era: components arrive via a forwarding barrel, composables arrive direct, so one package has two spellings decided by kind.

**Evidence.**

```
`wc -l demo/ui/*/index.ts | tail -1` -> 29 total across 19 directories; `grep -rn 'from "[./]*ui/[a-z-]*"' demo --include='*.vue' --include='*.ts' | wc -l` -> 90. Every barrel is a bare re-export, e.g. demo/ui/card/index.ts is one line: `export { Card, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription, CardContent, CardFooter } from "@mkbabb/glass-ui";`. ARCHITECTURE.md:39 forbids exactly this ('no ... one-line glass-ui forwarding directory'), and demo/ui/ is not in the declared tree — the declared home is shared/ui/ for 'only genuinely app-owned controls with 2+ consumers' (today EmptyState.vue, PaneHeader.vue). Picker sites: ColorPicker.vue:108, ColorComponentDisplay.vue:54, ComponentSliders.vue:95-96, ConsoleRail.vue:90. Meanwhile composables arrive direct: ColorPicker.vue:138 writeClipboard, SpectrumCanvas.vue:39 useTouchGate, App.vue:191 useClipboard.
```

**Reproduction.** cd /Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js && for d in demo/ui/*/; do echo "--- $d"; cat "$d"index.ts; done | head -60 && grep -rn 'from "[./]*ui/[a-z-]*"' demo --include='*.vue' --include='*.ts' | wc -l

**Proposed cure.** Delete all 19 directories and rewrite the 90 import sites to @mkbabb/glass-ui and its declared subpaths — a mechanical zero-behaviour change that removes a prohibited level.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-7 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** ColorPicker.vue holds a second, incorrect implementation of CSS hex validity, and is the fifth of eleven homes for the 'hex is a display encoding, not a space' special case.

**Mechanism.** Duplicate semantic ownership — colour syntax is uniquely the library's, and the display-space encoding policy has no single home, so both are re-decided per call site.

**Evidence.**

```
ColorPicker.vue:224-234 gates on /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}$/ before calling parseAndSetColor. Pasted run against the published parser: '#abc' regex true / library ok; '#abcd' true / ok; '#12345' true / library REJECT; '#abcdef' true / ok; '#1234567' true / library REJECT; '#abcdef12' true / ok. {3,8} admits the two lengths CSS does not define (5 and 7), so garbage passes to a parser that rejects it and triggers the 2s parseError flash (useColorParsing.ts:54-57). `grep -rn '=== "hex"|!== "hex"' demo` -> 11 sites: useColorPipeline.ts:118,176; useColorParsing.ts:66; useSliderGradients.ts:65; useColorNameResolution.ts:43; useColorUrl.ts:55; ColorSpaceSelector.vue:156; readoutReservation.ts:118; ComponentSliders.vue:161; ColorPicker.vue:224.
```

**Reproduction.** cd /Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js && node --input-type=module -e "const {parseCssColor}=await import('./dist/subpaths/css.js'); const re=/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}$/; for (const s of ['#abc','#abcd','#12345','#abcdef','#1234567','#abcdef12']) console.log(s, re.test(s), parseCssColor(s).ok);"

**Proposed cure.** Delete the regex; call the pipeline unconditionally and let the library's typed ParseResult decide. Give DisplayColorSpace one home — a color-session displaySpace module owning {resolve, format, parse, channels} for the hex encoding — collapsing 11 special cases to 1.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-8 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The component's real public surface is a 12-member imperative defineExpose handle reached by template ref; four members have zero consumers, one is never mutated, five are pure pipeline pass-throughs, and two are wired twice with divergent behaviour.

**Mechanism.** Service-locator public surface: a component instance stands in for an application service, so props/emits/slots stop being the contract and dead members accumulate unnoticed.

**Evidence.**

```
ColorPicker.vue:331-344. Consumer counts across demo/, test/, e2e/ (excluding the file itself): isTransitioning 0 (declared ref(false) at :330, never mutated anywhere in the repo), parseColor 0, setCurrentColor 0, editTarget 0 (duplicated by the update:editTarget emit at :276). Pass-throughs of inject(COLOR_MODEL_KEY): onPaletteApply, onPaletteAddColor, parseColor, setCurrentColor, applyExternalColor. commitEdit/cancelEdit are wired twice — App.vue:41-42 (which also sets viewManager.mobilePaneIndex.value = 1) and usePaneRouter.ts:156-157 (which does not). usePaletteWiring.ts:21 imports `type { ColorPicker }` purely to spell InstanceType<typeof ColorPicker>, making the leaf's instance type the app's contract.
```

**Reproduction.** cd /Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js && grep -rn 'isTransitioning' demo/ e2e/ test/ (2 hits, both in ColorPicker.vue) && grep -rn '\.parseColor|\.setCurrentColor|\.editTarget' demo/ e2e/ test/ | grep -v ColorPicker.vue (0 hits)

**Proposed cure.** defineExpose goes to zero once L-2 moves the edit session into color-session; the two commit/cancel wirings collapse to one owner.

The result is 31 uniform dark slabs with NO filled/unfilled split and no value cue except a 12px thumb — while the SAME variant on the picker's sliders renders a real gradient on the same page.

**Mechanism.** producer affordance thrown away + a per-instance override that defeats the variant it declares; a recorded mandate discharged by substituting a different cure

**Evidence.**

```
demo/scenes/ConfigSliderPane.vue:145 (`variant="spectrum"`) + :202 (`--slider-track-bg: var(--ink-muted, var(--muted-foreground))`). scratchpad/csp/p3.mjs on /#/blob: ConfigSliderPane track bg `oklch(0.447121 0.00386159 34.63)`, backgroundImage `none`, `.slider-range` backgroundColor `rgba(0,0,0,0)` + image `none`; picker population, same `data-variant=spectrum`, `--slider-track-bg: linear-gradient(to right, lab(0% 88.8 20) 0%, lab(10% 88.8 20) 10%, …)`. Visual: docs/tranches/V/megatranche/audit/visual/shots/forced-colors-desktop/blob.png frames both populations side by side. Unmet mandate: docs/tranches/N/waves/N.W13.md:96,215,295 — "Feed ConfigSliderPane.vue:139 the real --slider-track-bg gradient so its spectrum variant shows a spectrum." **Proposed cure.** Either feed the variant a real gradient over the slider's own domain (the honest reading of the N.W13 mandate, and what makes a 31-row console scannable), or stop declaring `spectrum` and consume the variant whose contract the pane actually wants. ### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defect hunt on `demo/scenes/Con` · C-4 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** `copyAsJson()` awaits `writeClipboard()` and discards its Result. `writeClipboard` never throws — it returns `{ok:false, reason:'no-api'|'clipboard-api'}`. Both documented failure modes are therefore completely silent: no text change, no aria-live, no disabled state, no toast. The success path is equally silent.

**Mechanism.** Result thrown away — a two-state producer contract collapsed to a fire-and-forget await

**Evidence.**

```
demo/scenes/ConfigSliderPane.vue:88-90. Producer contract: glass-ui/dist/useClipboard-D36OTaeT.js — returns `{ok:!1,reason:"no-api"}` when `navigator.clipboard?.writeText` is absent, `{ok:!1,reason:"clipboard-api"}` on rejection. Measured (scratchpad/csp/p3.mjs, both modes forced on the real button): noApi → {bodyTextChanged:false, anyAriaLiveInPane:0, buttonAriaDisabled:null, warns:[]}; rejected → {bodyTextChanged:false, warns:["[useClipboard] clipboard writeText rejected: NotAllowedError: Denied"]}. Success path measured at p2.mjs: visibleFeedbackTextAfter:false.
```

**Reproduction.** `node scratchpad/csp/p3.mjs` and read `clipboard`. Real-world: the repo sets `server.host:true` in vite.config.ts specifically to drive the demo from a phone over `http://<LAN-ip>:9000`; `navigator.clipboard` is secure-context-gated, so on that origin Copy JSON is a dead button on the exact surface the LAN host exists to serve.

**Proposed cure.** Consume the producer's `useClipboard()` — it already ships `status: 'idle'|'pending'|'success'|'failure'` and an `onCopyError(reason)` hook, and glass-ui also exports `toast`/`Toaster`. Bind status to the button and announce it in an `aria-live="polite"` region. Zero new components; edict 4 met by consumption rather than reinvention.

### `CHALLENGE-C — implementation defect hunt on `demo/scenes/Con` · C-5 · CHALLENGE-C

**Defect.** Unguarded dot-path read: `read()` casts a possibly-`undefined` path result to `number` and `fmt()` calls `.toFixed` on it during render. One absent optional intermediate segment throws mid-render and unmounts the ENTIRE pane through the error boundary — and the boundary swallows it so completely that nothing reaches `console` or `pageerror`.

**Mechanism.** stringly-typed reflection layer — an `as number` cast manufacturing an invariant the type system explicitly denies

**Evidence.**

```
demo/scenes/ConfigSliderPane.vue:57-86 (`readPath` returns undefined; `read` does `as number`; `fmt` does `v.toFixed(3)`); :66-73 (`writePath` has the mirror hole, walking through three unchecked casts). fmt boundary table measured in node: 0→"0", -0→"0", 0.5→"0.500", NaN→"NaN", Infinity→"Infinity", 1e21→"1e+21", undefined→THROWS, null→THROWS. Type permits the state: AuroraAtoms.zones is optional and the def is {key:"zones.count"} (AuroraPane.vue:103). Internal inconsistency: AuroraPane guards the same key 3 lines away — `atoms.zones?.arrangement ?? "composed"` (:74) and `atoms.zones?.count ?? 4` (:82).
```

**Reproduction.** INJECTED (labelled — not user-reachable through today's UI): `node scratchpad/csp/p4.mjs` sets the type-legal `config.zones = undefined` on the live reactive object at /#/atmosphere. Result: body text goes from the pane to "This panel hit an unexpected error. Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'toFixed')", configConsoleStillMounted:false, rowsAfter:0, pageErrors:[], consoleNoise:[]. The empty error arrays explain why REPORT.json reads 0 console/page errors on both routes in all four Safari matrices — this failure class is invisible to the visual harness by construction.

**Proposed cure.** Delete the reflection layer. Both consumers already double-cast their typed config to nothing (`(cfg as unknown) as Record<string, unknown>` — BlobPane.vue:124, AuroraPane.vue:111), so BlobPane's 13-line `NumericAtomPath` mapped type guards only the key literals while the indexed object is fully untyped. Give `SliderDef` its own `get: () => number` / `set: (n:number) => void` (or a typed lens): the class becomes unrepresentable and `readPath`/`writePath`/the `as number`/both `as unknown as` casts all disappear — strictly less code.

Census: all 19 demo/ui/* dirs are files=1 with 1-2 code lines, each a pure `export ... from "@mkbabb/glass-ui"` (alert, avatar, badge, button, card, checkbox, collapsible, dialog, dropdown-menu, input, label, popover, radio-group, select, separator, skeleton, slider, switch, tooltip). demo/ui/alert/index.ts:1-9 states the purpose verbatim: 'The two consumers (ColorNutritionLabel.vue, Markdown.vue) import from this barrel unchanged.' 92 import sites across demo/ route through the shims.
```

**Reproduction.** cd /Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js; for d in demo/ui/*/; do printf '%-28s files=%s codelines=%s\n' "$d" "$(find $d -type f|wc -l)" "$(grep -v '^\s*//' $d/index.ts|grep -v '^\s*$'|wc -l)"; done; cat demo/ui/alert/index.ts

**Proposed cure.** Delete demo/ui/ (19 files); rewrite the 92 import sites to `@mkbabb/glass-ui`. Purely mechanical. Evidence: `grep -rn '\"\\.\\./ui/' demo/` → 0 plus a green typecheck and gh-pages build.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-6 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** `useMarkdownColors` re-implements `useSafeAccentFn("resting")` by hand from three separate 4-hop reaches into `demo/color-session/` internals (which has NO index barrel/seam), and `resolveSurfaceLightnessLive` was widened onto color-session's surface specifically to serve that duplication. Same file also converts the library's failure-explicit Result back into thrown exceptions inside a computed.

**Mechanism.** one concept, two homes — a consumer that could not see the existing composable rebuilt it, and the library surface was widened to legitimise the rebuild rather than the call site corrected

**Evidence.**

```
useMarkdownColors.ts:5-7 → ../../../../color-session/keys (INK_AMBIENT_KEY), /ink (certifyAccentInk), /useContrastSafeColor (resolveSurfaceLightnessLive). `ls demo/color-session/index.ts` → No such file (no seam). demo/color-session/useContrastSafeColor.ts:346-361 (useSafeAccentFn) performs the identical three steps: useGlobalDark + inject(INK_AMBIENT_KEY) + certifyAccentInk(css, surfaceLightnessNow(surface, ambient, isDark)). useMarkdownColors.ts:18,25,44-73 hand-assembles the same. useContrastSafeColor.ts:365-368 docstring names the beneficiary: 'for composables that fold the referent into their own computation (`useMarkdownColors`)'. The only markdown-specific step is the chroma floor at useMarkdownColors.ts:52-53, which acts on the INPUT string not the referent. Throws inside computed at useMarkdownColors.ts:33, :37, :41.
```

**Reproduction.** cd /Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js; sed -n '340,370p' demo/color-session/useContrastSafeColor.ts; sed -n '1,10p;40,75p' demo/scenes/about/markdown/composables/useMarkdownColors.ts; ls demo/color-session/index.ts

**Proposed cure.** const { safeCss } = useSafeAccentFn("resting"); const accent = computed(() => safeCss(withChromaFloor(cssColor()))). 3 deep imports → 1; resolveSurfaceLightnessLive loses its named justification. Return {} on unparseable input instead of throwing — the --md-color-* vars are decoration and the inherited ink is the correct degenerate; a consumer that answers !parsed.ok with throw re-introduces the exception channel at the render boundary the library exists to eliminate. Give color-session a real index.ts seam.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-7 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** 142 of the 331 `<style scoped>` lines (42.9% of the block, 34.8% of the file) style markup that cannot exist — the component ships a GENERIC markdown theme against a CLOSED corpus of 11 hand-authored files using 8 element types, rendered by `Markdown({})` (plain markdown-it, no footnote/deflist/task-list/toc plugins).

**Mechanism.** speculative generality — a component named `Markdown` invited a general markdown theme, and rulings (AB-3 at :229-233, the F6 ONE-grammar law at :380-384) were adjudicated against markup that has never rendered, polluting the audit record

**Evidence.**

```
Static census across all 11 docs: `grep -c '^|'` → 0×11 (tables); `grep -c '^>'` → 0×11 (blockquotes); `grep -c '^!\['` → 0×11 (images); `grep -c '^```'` → 0×11 (fenced code); heading levels h1=0 h2=53 h3=85 h4=0 h5=0 h6=0. Confirmed in the LIVE DOM (Playwright at http://localhost:9000/#/, querySelectorAll inside .markdown-wrapper .markdown-body, 34 children): {table:0,th:0,td:0,blockquote:0,img:0,dl:0,dt:0,dd:0,'ul.contains-task-list':0,'li.task-list-item':0,'.callout':0,'.footnotes':0,'.footnote-ref':0,'.footnote-item':0,'.toc':0,pre:0,a:0, hr:4,code:14,'mark.cs-name':9,'div.inline-block':9,'.katex':9,'.katex-display':4}. Dead ranges in Markdown.vue: 142-146 (h5/h6 rhythm), 149-155 (14 of 15 adjacency selectors), 157-159 (h1), 171-174 (h4), 176-178 (h5), 180-182 (h6), 229-238 (pre + AB-3 comment), 255-260 (blockquote), 262-276 (table/th/td), 278-283 (img), 316-329 (dl/dt/dd), 331-342 (task lists), 344-357 (callout), 359-378 (footnotes), 380-406 (toc) = 142 lines. Also: `.markdown-wrapper :deep(.markdown-body)` duplicated at :104 and :198 for no reason; Markdown.vue:94-96 claims --phi-* live in 'style.css :root' but `find demo -name style.css` → nothing (they are at demo/styles/foundation.css:458-460). vite.config.ts:162 = `Markdown({})`.
```

**Reproduction.** cd /Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js; for f in assets/docs/*.md; do printf '%s tables=%s quotes=%s imgs=%s fences=%s\n' $f $(grep -c '^|' $f) $(grep -c '^>' $f) $(grep -c '^!\[' $f) $(grep -c '^```' $f); done; for lvl in '#' '##' '###' '####' '#####' '######'; do echo -n "$lvl "; grep -h "^$lvl " assets/docs/*.md | wc -l; done

**Proposed cure.** Excise the 142 dead lines and extract the surviving ~90 to demo/styles/markdown.css as ONE global `.md-prose` class (h2 h3 p ul ol li code hr a + the katex-display scroll box) — unscoped, no :deep(). Styling a document is a stylesheet's job, not a component's. Close with a DOM golden asserting styled-selector-set == rendered-selector-set, so speculative CSS cannot re-accumulate.

### `CHALLENGE-L — library structure: module boundaries, ownershi` · L-8 · CHALLENGE-L

**Defect.** The component calls a glass-ui API that does not exist: `<Skeleton surface="glass" variant="shimmer">` ×3. glass-ui 7.0.0's Skeleton declares exactly one prop (`class`) and its compiled CSS carries a single unconditional recipe with no variant selectors. The props fall through as inert non-standard HTML attributes. Nothing catches it because strictTemplates is off (L-2).

**Mechanism.** a design intent expressed through a non-existent prop API; it looks correct only because shimmer is glass-ui's unconditional default, so the props are noise that would keep 'working' if glass-ui changed it — and the gate that should catch it is disabled

**Evidence.**

```
Markdown.vue:2-8 (comment 'glass shimmer bones, not opaque pulse blocks' + 3 call sites with surface/variant). node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui/dist/components/skeleton/Skeleton.vue.d.ts: `type __VLS_Props = { class?: HTMLAttributes["class"] };`. Compiled CSS (glass-ui.css): `.skeleton[data-v-cd03d0b0]{isolation:isolate;border-radius:var(--radius-input);background:var(--muted);position:relative;overflow:hidden}` plus two unconditional ::after rules — no [surface=]/[variant=]/.shimmer selectors exist (`grep -o 'skeleton[^{,]*\|shimmer[^{,]*' glass-ui.css | sort -u` → skeleton, skeleton-scan-cd03d0b0, skeleton-title, .skeleton[data-v-cd03d0b0], :after only). `grep -n 'strictTemplates' tsconfig*.json` → no match. Second-order: glass-ui fills the skeleton with var(--muted) — the exact tone this component's own style block (Markdown.vue:229-232) rules must NEVER be used ('never the parallel --muted species, which stepped OPPOSITE the well in dark').
```

**Reproduction.** cd /Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js; cat node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui/dist/components/skeleton/Skeleton.vue.d.ts; grep -o '\.skeleton\[data-v-cd03d0b0\][^}]*}' node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui/dist/glass-ui.css; sed -n '2,8p' demo/scenes/about/markdown/Markdown.vue; grep -n 'strictTemplates' tsconfig*.json

**Proposed cure.** Enable vueCompilerOptions.strictTemplates (makes this a compile error), then delete the two dead props. If the skeleton genuinely must seat on --well-bg rather than --muted, that is a glass-ui change per edict 4 — a variant added in glass-ui, never a demo per-instance override.

### `design` · D-4 · design

**Defect.** A certified text-ink token (`--ink-muted`, specified at >=4.5:1 for glyph mass) is used as a ~23,000px2 area fill, making the inert track the pane's heaviest and near-loudest object.

**Mechanism.** Text ink is specified for a few-hundred-px2 glyph mass; applied to continuous surface it is a different optical object — it reads as a painted bar, not a groove at adequate contrast. Violates VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:14-16 (tier separation: instrument veil vs specimen well) and :21 ("Dark chrome uses the restrained neutral pole"). PROPORTION-AUDIT.md:73 law 8: "Real rendered relation wins over token intent ... token presence alone cannot close a row" — the comment at :190-201 closes its row on token presence.

**Evidence.**

```
ConfigSliderPane.vue:202 `--slider-track-bg: var(--ink-muted, var(--muted-foreground))`. Measured (chD-csp-probe2.mjs): track vs well 5.82:1 light / 8.30:1 dark; row label vs well 13.52:1 / 9.28:1; section heading 5.08:1 / 5.97:1. In dark mode the inert track sits within one stop of the label. Track geometry 962x24 on /atmosphere, 432x24 on /blob.
```

**Reproduction.** node scratchpad/chD-csp-probe2.mjs -> contrast-light / contrast-dark

**Proposed cure.** Fold into D-3. The track's material belongs to the producer Slider's neutral recipe at its own graphics rung; if that rung is genuinely below 3:1 on --well-bg, the fix is a glass-ui `--slider-track-*` token pair sized for area, not a consumer re-ink borrowed from the text ladder.

### `design` · D-5 · design

**Defect.** 31 slider thumbs render 12x24px — below the WCAG 2.5.8 24x24 minimum on the inline axis — and the component's hit-area extension is gated to `@media (pointer: coarse)`, so desktop is uncovered.

**Mechanism.** WCAG 2.5.8 is not pointer-type-conditional. The coarse-only guard leaves pointer:fine uncovered, and on /blob the coarse arm is dead code anyway because the pane is not rendered at coarse-pointer widths (D-2). PROPORTION-AUDIT.md:72 law 7 explicitly licenses extending the operable target without growing the visual glyph.

**Evidence.**

```
REPORT.json safari-desktop-light /#/blob a11y.smallTapTargets: 39 entries, 31 of them exactly {w:12,h:24,tag:"span"} labelled with every BlobPane slider (Body Radius ... Speed). Identical in safari-desktop-dark. Live: thumb {w:12,h:24} at both 1440 and 390. Cure gate: ConfigSliderPane.vue:218-230. Mobile arm proves the cure works (REPORT.json safari-mobile-light channel spans h:44; /atmosphere mobile rows h 57.59).
```

**Reproduction.** http://localhost:9000/#/blob at 1440px; inspect any [role="slider"] inside .config-console — 12x24 CSS px.

**Proposed cure.** Delete the `pointer: coarse` guard and move the hit extension onto the glass-ui Slider thumb (unconditional), retiring the consumer `:deep()` block entirely. PROPORTION-AUDIT.md:56 PR-12 already owns this family at W18.

**Defect.** The pane emits 8 dividing lines on /blob where the binding boundary inventory specifies `none`, and the section rule is scheme-asymmetric: invisible in light (1.08:1), a hard rule in dark (5.03:1).

**Mechanism.** Duplicative boundary: PROPORTION-AUDIT.md:69 law 4 retains a divider only when grouping would be ambiguous without it, and the pane already separates sections with gap-5 plus a distinct heading. `color-mix(in srgb, var(--border) 50%, transparent)` over --well-bg is invisible in one scheme and crisp in the other, a 4.7x swing for one element. Under forced colors this forbidden rule is the only surviving element in the pane (D-1) — a precise inversion of design priority.

Live /#/blob: sectionHeaders 7, actionBars 1 = 8 lines. Sources: ConfigSliderPane.vue:232-235 (.config-section-header border-bottom, one per section) and :245-251 (.config-action-bar border-top). OPTICAL-BENCH-COMPOSITIONS.md:80-81 gives Atmosphere and Blob `Retained non-P122 dividing line: none`; :90 "Any additional line ... is a defect". Measured divider vs well: 1.08:1 light, 5.03:1 dark (chD-csp-probe2.mjs).

### `design` · D-7 · design

**Defect.** A section heading and a control label are computed-identical (6/6 properties), the section heading is the same size as the row labels it groups, and the closed type matrix is breached in three roles.

**Mechanism.** VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:66-78 is a closed type matrix. Section heading must be text-heading/Plus Jakarta Sans — rendered Fira Code 16.4px caps. Control label must be text-small PJS non-bold — rendered Fira Code caps in the slot rows and PJS weight 500 in the well rows. Value must be text-mono-small or mono-caption — rendered text-micro 11px, neither token. Mono is thus louder as a heading (16.4px) than as the data it groups (11px), and FIELD vs HARMONY are indistinguishable in the frame.

**Evidence.**

```
/#/atmosphere 1440x900 (chD-csp-probe3.mjs -> twoSpecies.identicalRecipe): .config-section-title (ConfigSliderPane.vue:237-243) vs .aurora-row-label (AuroraPane.vue:194-199) — fontFamily, fontSize (16.4px), textTransform (uppercase), letterSpacing (1.64px), color rgb(112,89,66), fontWeight 400 — all six equal, and the two CSS blocks are byte-identical in five declarations in the same order. Sizes: section title 16.4px == row label 16.4px (desktop) and 14px == 14px (390px); value 11px at both. Label/value ratio drifts 1.49 (desktop) -> 1.27 (mobile).
```

**Reproduction.** node scratchpad/chD-csp-probe3.mjs -> twoSpecies.identicalRecipe / .secTitles / .rowLabels; visual: shots/safari-mobile-dark/atmosphere.png (FIELD and HARMONY render identically).

**Proposed cure.** One matrix line per role: section headings -> text-heading (Plus Jakarta Sans), restoring a real size step above the row label; the mono/caps/tracking recipe survives in exactly one place, the value/provenance voice. The duplicated .aurora-row-label block dies with the slot cure (D-13). The value's size token moves to text-mono-small and rides the same container clamp as the label so the pair holds its ratio.

### `design` · D-8 · design

**Defect.** The pane roots a glass Card containing a second bordered, radiused opaque sub-card, on two compositions whose binding rows specify Card count 0 and forbid a settings Card stack by name.

**Mechanism.** A configuration form is a page region, not a bounded specimen. VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:19 "One surface has one tier. An inner card is not automatically another pane of glass." PROPORTION-AUDIT.md:66 law 1 "a mere padding group does not become a Card by default." foundation.css:339-348 concedes the point: .console-well is an "INTERIM demo class — swaps onto the producer .glass-well rung when packet P3 ships", i.e. a re-minted producer primitive with the swap booked rather than met (edict 4 deferred).

**Evidence.**

```
ConfigSliderPane.vue:17 imports Card (demo/ui/card/index.ts is a one-line glass-ui re-export); :99-101 `<Card tier="resting" ...>` is the pane root; :122 nests .console-well (demo/styles/foundation.css:350-354: background --well-bg, 1px solid --card-edge, --radius-panel). Live ancestor walk from .console-well on /#/blob: [{tag:"DIV", m:["glass-resting"]}], wells: 1. OPTICAL-BENCH-COMPOSITIONS.md:67 "the other sixteen compositions have Card count `0`"; :46 Atmosphere "form sections not Cards"; :47 Blob "no settings Card stack".

### `design` · D-9 · design

**Defect.** The canon-required essentials/advanced/compare composition is unrepresentable: `SliderSection` has no disclosure axis, so 31 rows ship flat in a 695px window behind a hard clip with no fade.

**Mechanism.** The API makes the canonical composition impossible for any consumer, which is the difference between a missing feature and a design defect. The `maskImage:none` measurement also falsifies the component's own comment at :104-106 ("The scroll region owns the fade mask") and the class name pane-scroll-fade — that class sets only `contain` and `scroll-timeline`. Visible consequence: shots/safari-mobile-dark/atmosphere.png shows `Noise 0.500` with its control amputated at a hard edge.

**Evidence.**

```
ConfigSliderPane.vue:27-39 — `SliderDef {key,label,min,max,step}`, `SliderSection {title,defs}`; no rank/collapsed/compare/disabled/unit/format. Live /#/blob: scrollHeight 2605, clientHeight 695 (3.7 screens), maskImage "none", webkitMaskImage "none", scroll-region bottom 799 == action-bar top 799. OPTICAL-BENCH-COMPOSITIONS.md:46-47 names the regions (Atmosphere preview;essentials;advanced — Blob preview;essentials;advanced/reset/compare); VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:51-52,214; PROPORTION-AUDIT.md:54 PR-10.
```

**Reproduction.** node scratchpad/chD-csp-probe2.mjs -> scrollRegion; visual: http://localhost:9000/#/blob at 1440x900, bottom of the well.

**Proposed cure.** `SliderSection` gains a disclosure disposition — `{title, rank: "essential"|"advanced", defs}` — with advanced sections composed into glass-ui's existing configurator disclosure surface (the producer already ships .configurator-layer-region with a PRM-guarded grid-template-rows transition). `compare` and a real reset land as the pane's action region per OPTICAL-BENCH-COMPOSITIONS.md:47.

### `design` · D-10 · design

**Defect.** Two actions with zero result state: Copy JSON has no success, failure or pending truth (and swallows rejections), and Reset is destructive, unconfirmed, irreversible, with no compare.

**Mechanism.** VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:101 "Persistent operation state stays with the entity/workspace. A transient flourish ... never carries the only truth" — here there is no truth at all; PROPORTION-AUDIT.md:52 PR-08 is the standing row. The whole interaction grammar (VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:96) is select -> tune -> commit; this pane has no commit stage — every drag writes straight into the injected config (:81) and Reset is the only exit. OPTICAL-BENCH-COMPOSITIONS.md:47 requires a `compare` region that does not exist.

**Evidence.**

```
ConfigSliderPane.vue:88-94. Live /#/blob: actions.btns = [{text:"Copy JSON", aria:null, disabled:false},{text:"Reset", aria:null, disabled:false}]; actions.liveRegions finds only the Picker's four channel meters at aria-live="off" — the pane emits no status region at all. `@click="copyAsJson"` on an async function means a writeClipboard rejection is an unhandled promise rejection; every other demo call site marks intent with `void writeClipboard(...)` (ColorPicker.vue:325, ColorInput.vue:222, usePaletteActions.ts:134).
```

**Reproduction.** http://localhost:9000/#/blob -> deny clipboard permission -> click Copy JSON: nothing happens, no error surfaces. Click Reset: 31 values change with no confirmation, announcement or undo.

**Proposed cure.** One durable status region in the action bar owned by the pane (VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:101); Reset becomes Reset + Compare with the canon's destructive-confirmation grammar; copyAsJson gains explicit success/failure arms.

### `design` · D-11 · design

**Defect.** The live readout has neither tabular figures nor reserved width, and infers precision from the value instead of the statically-known step — producing a ~27px live width jitter mid-drag on nearly every slider.

**Mechanism.** Precision derived from the value, not the def. Every BlobPane slider whose range contains an integer (warpAmp 0-1, eccentricity 0-0.5, noiseFreq 0.5-10.0, pulseFreq 0-2, specStrength 0-2, rimPower 1-5, iridescence/sssScale/coreGlow 0-1, pointerAttraction -1..1, stretch 0-1.5, tempo 0.25-2.5, ...) flips its readout between 5 chars and 1 char mid-drag — a ~80% width swing on a 33.9px element. `noiseFreq` step 0.1 renders `0.500`, two digits of false precision on 8 of 31 rows. No `unit` field exists, so `Merge (ms)` smuggles its unit into the label while `Hue Range` (degrees) has none, against VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:124 ("announce label, value, unit").

**Evidence.**

```
Live /#/blob: readout fontVariantNumeric "normal"; "3" = 6.8px, "0.220" = 33.9px, "0.038" = 33.9px; in-place swap "0.220"->"-0.0005" = 32.1 -> 43.7px (delta +11.6px). ConfigSliderPane.vue:84-86 `fmt(v) = Number.isInteger(v) ? String(v) : v.toFixed(3)`. VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:78: "Live numbers use tabular figures and reserve their widest legal representation so value changes never reflow the settled chassis."
```

**Reproduction.** node scratchpad/chD-csp-probe2.mjs -> truncation.readoutWidthShift; chD-csp-probe.mjs -> reflow. Visually: drag Warp on /#/blob through 1.00.

**Proposed cure.** `SliderDef` declares `precision` (or derives it once from `step`) and `unit`; the readout gets font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums and a min-inline-size reserved from the widest legal string. Both belong on glass-ui's ConfiguratorRow value slot — VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:104 already assigns the axis composition ("label, unit, reserved live value, ...") to the producer.

---

### `design` · D-12 · design

**Defect.** The declared "ONE RHYTHM SOURCE" container clamp is measurably inert — rendered row height is identical at container widths differing by 2.03x — and it bypasses the producer's real sizing API with utility classes plus a :deep() correction.

**Mechanism.** The row content box (label line + gap-1.5 + 24px track + py-1) always exceeds the clamp, so the clamp never binds and the claim at :208-214 is false — the rhythm is fixed, not container-scaled, contrary to VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:33 law 7. The actual spacing comes from `class="gap-1.5 py-1"` (:142), replacing the producer's ladder, plus a `:deep()` descendant correction that VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:89 says W17 deletes.

**Evidence.**

```
ConfigSliderPane.vue:208-217 `min-block-size: clamp(2rem, 7cqi, 2.625rem)`. Measured (chD-csp-probe.mjs): /atmosphere container 1042px -> resolved 42px, rendered row 60.94px; /blob container 512px -> resolved 35.84px, rendered row 60.94px; /atmosphere 390px container 358px -> 32px, rendered 57.59px. distinctRowHeights = [60.9] on both desktop arms. Separately dataSize: null on every row, while glass-ui.css ships `.configurator-row[data-size=sm|md|lg]` with --configurator-row-gap-* / --configurator-row-py-* plus an @container style(--configurator-size) seam.
```

**Reproduction.** node scratchpad/chD-csp-probe.mjs -> compare desktop-atmosphere.row0 / desktop-blob.row0 / mobile-atmosphere.row0

**Proposed cure.** Delete :215-217 and :142. Pass the producer's `size` (or set --configurator-size once on .config-console) and let the producer's gap/padding ladder own the rhythm. If the rhythm must be container-scaled, that clamp belongs in the producer's --configurator-row-py-* tokens so every consumer inherits one law — which is what "ONE RHYTHM SOURCE" was meant to mean.

### `design` · D-13 · design

**Defect.** The bare unnamed `<slot />` licenses a second control-row species in the same pane: 15px different indent, 1.69x different height, two type families at two weights and two colours, on two material tiers, with ragged control columns.

**Mechanism.** The pane owns a row grammar and a heading grammar and exposes neither to the slot, so the consumer re-minted the nearest recipe it could see — the duplication is the tell. Violates VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:33 law 7 (no fork), :104 (one producer axis composition) and edict 4 (glass-ui is the design system; do not re-mint a row). ConfigSliderPane.vue:3 also documents a prop `extraControls?` that :41-52 never declares — the API drifted from its own header comment.

**Evidence.**

```
ConfigSliderPane.vue:110 (bare slot); AuroraPane.vue:118-181 fills it. Measured /#/atmosphere 1440x900 (chD-csp-probe3.mjs): .aurora-row left 224 right 1216 height 36px, label Fira Code 400 uppercase rgb(112,89,66), on the Card, controls `space-between` so four triggers form no column; .configurator-row left 239 right 1201 height 60.9px, label Plus Jakarta Sans 500 normal-case rgb(28,25,23), inside .console-well. .aurora-row-label (AuroraPane.vue:194-199) is a byte-copy of .config-section-title (ConfigSliderPane.vue:237-243).
```

**Reproduction.** node scratchpad/chD-csp-probe3.mjs -> twoSpecies.auroraRows vs twoSpecies.cwRows, twoSpecies.auroraLabels vs twoSpecies.rowLabels; visual: shots/safari-desktop-light/atmosphere.png.

**Proposed cure.** The slot must expose rows, not a blank region: a `sections` entry accepts an enum/select def alongside slider defs so every control in the pane is one ConfiguratorRow, in one grid, one material tier, one indent. AuroraPane.vue:118-181 and its whole <style> block delete with it — a real subtraction, no new directory and no wrapper component (edict 3).

### `design` · D-14 · design

**Defect.** The spectral-meniscus material — reserved by the constitution for genuinely chromatic domains (Picker and Gradient only) — is applied unconditionally to 34 geometry, material and time axes.

**Mechanism.** Applying the reserved signature form to non-chromatic domains devalues the signature, and — because the variant blanks .slider-range by recipe — is the direct cause of D-3's missing value read. The record shows it was diagnosed twice and cured neither time: docs/tranches/N/audit/lanes2/U-CONTROLS.md:54 and docs/tranches/N/waves/N.W13.md:28 ("a spectrum slider with no spectrum"); the T.W8 cure at :190-202 fed the variant an ink instead of retiring the variant.

**Evidence.**

```
ConfigSliderPane.vue:146 `variant="spectrum"` for all 31 BlobPane defs and all 3 AuroraPane defs; live `data-variant="spectrum"` on every .glass-slider in .config-console. VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:7: "the spectral meniscus: a continuous liquid-color rail reserved for genuinely chromatic continuous domains — Picker and Gradient." Domains actually driven: Body Radius, Smooth K, Rim Power, Shininess, Merge (ms), Zones.
```

**Reproduction.** http://localhost:9000/#/blob; inspect data-variant on any .config-console .glass-slider

**Proposed cure.** Retire variant="spectrum" from this pane and consume the producer's neutral Slider (see D-3). The spectral meniscus returns to Picker and Gradient only.

### `design` · D-15 · design

**Defect.** Dependent-control state was never designed: at Satellites = 0, five sliders control nothing yet remain fully enabled, focusable and draggable with live readouts.

**Mechanism.** VISUAL-CONSTITUTION.md:83: "Selected, failed, pending, withdrawn and disabled states are never color-only. Role, accessible name, state/value and associated error/status are explicit." There is no disabled state to be colour-only with — the state does not exist in the model, so five controls silently lie about their effect.

**Evidence.**

```
BlobPane.vue:59-62 and :111-112 — satelliteCount range 0..4; satelliteRadius, orbitRadius, eccentricity, mergeDuration, emergeDuration all satellite-dependent. ConfigSliderPane.vue:27-33 `SliderDef` has no `disabled` field and the template renders no disabled state; live probe shows aria-disabled: null on every thumb.
```

**Reproduction.** http://localhost:9000/#/blob at >=1024px -> drag Satellites to 0 -> drag Sat Radius, Orbit Radius, Eccentricity, Merge (ms), Emerge (ms): readouts change, the preview does not, and no control indicates it is inert.

**Proposed cure.** `SliderDef` gains `enabledWhen?: (config) => boolean` (or a declarative `dependsOn` path); the row renders the producer's disabled state with aria-disabled plus an explicit reason. Consumed once by glass-ui's ConfiguratorRow, not hand-rolled per consumer.
